def canonical(v):return json.dumps(v,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False,allow_nan=False)
def validate(root,lane,h,index):
 if canonical(h)!=canonical(FIXED):raise ValueError('complete typed handoff/source/ROOT/predicate/authority claims')
 if canonical(index)!=canonical(INDEX):raise ValueError('complete exact decidingoriginal index')
 if type(index['copied_files']) is not int or type(index['copied_bytes']) is not int or index['copied_files']!=len(index['copies']) or index['copied_bytes']!=sum(r['bytes'] for r in index['copies']):raise ValueError('recomputed copiedsummary')
 expected=set(h['handoff_footprint']);actual={str(p.relative_to(lane)) for p in root.rglob('*') if p.is_file()}
 if actual!=expected or len(actual)!=h['handoff_path_count'] or h['complete_publication_union_count']!=207+len(actual):raise ValueError('fullhandoff footprint')
 for r in index['copies']:
  rel=Path(r['portable_path'])
  if rel.is_absolute() or '..' in rel.parts or str(rel).endswith('.py'):raise ValueError('safe passive route')
  b=(root/rel).read_bytes();original=Path(r['source_path']).read_bytes()
  if b!=original or type(r['bytes']) is not int or len(b)!=r['bytes'] or hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()!=r['sha256']:raise ValueError('literal decidingcopy content')
 for p in root.rglob('*.json'):json.loads(p.read_bytes())
 tree=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse',h['candidate_sha']+'^{tree}'],cwd=lane,text=True).strip()
 if tree!=h['candidate_tree_sha']:raise ValueError('actual candidate tree')
 paths=subprocess.check_output(['git','diff','--name-only',h['base_sha'],h['candidate_sha']],cwd=lane,text=True).splitlines()
 if set(paths)!={r['path'] for r in h['implementation_footprint']} or len(paths)!=207:raise ValueError('actual wholeimplementation footprint')
 for r in h['implementation_footprint']:
  b=subprocess.check_output(['git','show',h['candidate_sha']+':'+r['path']],cwd=lane)
  if len(b)!=r['bytes'] or hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()!=r['sha256']:raise ValueError('actual immutable source bytes')
 return {'outcome':'PASS exact DOC handoff custody','copies':index['copied_files'],'copied_bytes':index['copied_bytes'],'handoff_paths':len(actual),'source_paths':207,'ROOT_whole_input_immutable':False,'source_native_numeric_gate_execution':False}
def main():
 a=argparse.ArgumentParser();a.add_argument('--root',required=True);a.add_argument('--lane',required=True);args=a.parse_args();root=Path(args.root);lane=Path(args.lane)
 print(json.dumps(validate(root,lane,json.loads((root/'implementation-handoff.json').read_bytes()),json.loads((root/'copy-index.json').read_bytes())),indent=2))
if __name__=='__main__':main()
